Inclusion(sec 8) ^4pVUIi7B

- salary, wages, overtime payment ,(pension excluded in 2082/83)

- employer's contribution to retirement fund or provident fund, leave encashment fee, gratuity and any other retirement payment received during the job period ^6neJO72g

1. Quantified value of asset transferred [market value on date of transfer]
2. Personal vehicle (four wheeler) facility [0.5% of salary plus grade or actual amount paid by employer]
3. Accommodation facility [2% of salary plus grade or actual amount paid by employer in cash or kind]
4. Loan facility [interest as per market value - interest paid by employee]
5. Other perquisites [ amount paid by employer - amount paid by employee]
6. Amount paid to third party [ amount paid by employer on request of employee]
7. Uniform/dress allowance [with logo.       Not included.     Without logo.     Included]
8. Meal allowance
9. Other reimbursement of personal expense of employee ^gGmL0iMX
